## Narration 876

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A914)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا يَعْقُوبُ، - يَعْنِي ابْنَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ - عَنْ سُهَيْلٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِمَعْنَى حَدِيثِ سُمَىٍّ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 914]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/914" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सुहैल ने अपने वालिद (अबू सालेह) से, उन्हों ने हज़रत अबू हुरैराह (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से और उन्हों ने नबी (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से (मज़कूरा बाला रावी) सुमय की हदीस के मानिंद रिवायत की।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 914]</p>

<h3>Urdu</h3>

## Narration 877

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A915)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ يَحْيَى، قَالَ قَرَأْتُ عَلَى مَالِكٍ عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَأَبِي، سَلَمَةَ بْنِ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ أَنَّهُمَا أَخْبَرَاهُ عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏&quot;‏ إِذَا أَمَّنَ الإِمَامُ فَأَمِّنُوا فَإِنَّهُ مَنْ وَافَقَ تَأْمِينُهُ تَأْمِينَ الْمَلاَئِكَةِ غُفِرَ لَهُ مَا تَقَدَّمَ مِنْ ذَنْبِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ قَالَ ابْنُ شِهَابٍ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يَقُولُ ‏&quot;‏ آمِينَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 915]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/915" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 915</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   915 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: SayAmin when the Imam says Amin, for it anyone&#x27;s utterance of Amin synchronises with that of the angels, he will be forgiven his past sins</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 915]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/915" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   915 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मालिक ने इब्न-ए-शिहाब से, उन्हों ने सईद बिन मुसय्यब और अबू सलामह बिन अब्दुर रहमान से रिवायत की के उन दोनों ने हज़रत अबू हुरैराह (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जब इमाम &#x27;आमीन&#x27; कहे तो तुम भी &#x27;आमीन&#x27; कहो क्यूंके जिस की &#x27;आमीन&#x27; फ़रिश्तों की &#x27;आमीन&#x27; के मुवाफ़िक़ हो जाये गी, उस के सबक़ा गुनाह माफ़ कर दिए जाएंगे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 915]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/915" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">مالک نے ابن شہاب سے ، انہوں نے سعید بن مسیب اور ابو سلمہ بن عبد الرحمن سے روایت کی کہ ان دونوں نے حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی کہ رسول اللہﷺ نے فرمایا : ’’ جب امام آمین کہے تو تم بھی آمین کہو کیونکہ جس کی آمین فرشتوں کی آمین کے موافق ہو جائے گی ، اس کے سابقہ گناہ معاف کر دیے جائیں گے ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 915]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/915" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Malik ne Ibn-e-Shihab se, unhon ne Saeed bin Musayyab aur Abu Salamah bin Abdur Rehman se riwayat ki ke un dono ne Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jab Imam &#x27;Aameen&#x27; kahe to tum bhi &#x27;Aameen&#x27; kaho kyunke jis ki &#x27;Aameen&#x27; farishton ki &#x27;Aameen&#x27; ke muwafiq ho jaye gi, us ke sabqa gunah maaf kar diye jayenge.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 915]</p>

## Narration 878

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A916)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي حَرْمَلَةُ بْنُ يَحْيَى، أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ وَهْبٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي يُونُسُ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي ابْنُ الْمُسَيَّبِ، وَأَبُو سَلَمَةَ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ أَنَّ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ سَمِعْتُ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏.‏ بِمِثْلِ حَدِيثِ مَالِكٍ وَلَمْ يَذْكُرْ قَوْلَ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 916]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/916" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 916</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   916 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira said: I heard from the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) the hadith like one transmitted by Malik, but he made no mention of the words of Shihab</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 916]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/916" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">(मालिक के बजाय) यूनुस ने इब्न-ए-शिहाब से, उन्हों ने इब्न-ए-मुसय्यब और अबू सलामह बिन अब्दुर रहमान से रिवायत की के हज़रत अबू हुरैराह (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: मैं ने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से सुना... (आगे) मालिक की मज़कूरा बाला रिवायत की तरह है, अलबत्ता यूनुस ने इब्न-ए-शिहाब का क़ौल बयान नहीं किया।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 916]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/916" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">۔ ( مالک کے بجائے ) یونس نے ابن شہاب سے ، انہوں نے ابن مسیب اور ابو سلمہ بن عبد الرحمن سے روایت کی کہ حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ نے کہا : میں نے رسول اللہﷺ سے سنا ... ( آگے ) مالک کی مذکورہ بالا روایت کی طرح ہے ، البتہ یونس نے ابن شہاب کا قول بیان نہیں کیا ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 916]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/916" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">(Malik ke bajaye) Younus ne Ibn-e-Shihab se, unhon ne Ibn-e-Musayyab aur Abu Salamah bin Abdur Rehman se riwayat ki ke Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Main ne Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se suna... (aage) Malik ki mazkoora baala riwayat ki tarah hai, albatta Younus ne Ibn-e-Shihab ka qaul bayan nahi kiya.</p>

## Narration 879

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A917)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي حَرْمَلَةُ بْنُ يَحْيَى، حَدَّثَنِي ابْنُ وَهْبٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي عَمْرٌو، أَنَّ أَبَا يُونُسَ، حَدَّثَهُ عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا قَالَ أَحَدُكُمْ فِي الصَّلاَةِ آمِينَ ‏.‏ وَالْمَلاَئِكَةُ فِي السَّمَاءِ آمِينَ ‏.‏ فَوَافَقَ إِحْدَاهُمَا الأُخْرَى غُفِرَ لَهُ مَا تَقَدَّمَ مِنْ ذَنْبِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 917]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/917" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 917</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   917 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: When anyone amongst you utters Amin in prayer and the angels in the sky also utter Amin, and this (utterance of the one) synchronises with (that of) the other, all his previous sins are pardoned</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 917]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/917" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 917</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   917 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबु यूनुस (सलीम बिन जुबैर) ने हज़रात अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से हदीस बयान की के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जब तुम में से कोई नमाज़ में &#x27;आमीन&#x27; कहे और फरिश्ते आसमान में &#x27;आमीन&#x27; कहें और एक &#x27;आमीन&#x27; दूसरी के मुवाफ़िक़ हो जाये तो उस के पिछले गुनाह माफ कर दिए जाते हैं.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 917]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/917" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Hindi · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 917</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   917 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو یونس ( سلیم بن جبیر ) نے حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے حدیث بیان کی کہ رسول اللہﷺ نے فرمایا : ’’ جب تم میں سے کوئی نماز میں آمین کہے اور فرشتے آسمان میں آمین کہیں اور ایک آمین دوسری کے موافق ہو جائے تو اس کے پچھلے گناہ معاف کر دیے جاتے ہیں ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 917]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/917" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Younus (Saleem bin Jubair) ne Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se hadees bayan ki ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jab tum mein se koi namaz mein &#x27;Aameen&#x27; kahe aur farishtay aasman mein &#x27;Aameen&#x27; kahein aur ek &#x27;Aameen&#x27; dusri ke muwafiq ho jaye to us ke pichle gunah maaf kar diye jaate hain.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 917]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/917" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 880

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A918)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مَسْلَمَةَ الْقَعْنَبِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا الْمُغِيرَةُ، عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا قَالَ أَحَدُكُمْ آمِينَ ‏.‏ وَالْمَلاَئِكَةُ فِي السَّمَاءِ آمِينَ ‏.‏ فَوَافَقَتْ إِحْدَاهُمَا الأُخْرَى غُفِرَ لَهُ مَا تَقَدَّمَ مِنْ ذَنْبِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 918]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/918" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Harare reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: When anyone amongst you utters Amin and the angels In the heaven also utter Amin and (the Amin) of the one synchronises with (that of) the other, all his previous sins are pardoned</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 918]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/918" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हज़रात अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के एक और शागिर्द आ&#x27;राज के हवाले से रिवायत है के अबु हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जब तुम में से एक शख्स &#x27;आमीन&#x27; कहे और फरिश्ते आसमान में &#x27;आमीन&#x27; कहें और &#x27;आमीन&#x27; दूसरी के मुवाफ़िक़ हो जाये तो उस शख्स के पिछले गुनाह माफ कर दिए जाते हैं.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 918]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/918" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ کے ایک اور شاگرد اعرج کے حوالے سے روایت ہے کہ ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ نے کہا : رسول اللہﷺ نے فرمایا : ’’ جب تم میں سے ایک شخص آمین کہے اور فرشتے آسمان میں آمین کہیں اور آمین دوسری کے موافق ہو جائے تو اس شخص کے پچھلے گناہ معاف کر دیے جاتے ہیں</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 918]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/918" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke ek aur shagird A&#x27;raj ke hawalay se riwayat hai ke Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jab tum mein se ek shakhs &#x27;Aameen&#x27; kahe aur farishtay aasman mein &#x27;Aameen&#x27; kahein aur &#x27;Aameen&#x27; dusri ke muwafiq ho jaye to us shakhs ke pichle gunah maaf kar diye jaate hain.</p>

## Narration 881

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A919)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ رَافِعٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّزَّاقِ، حَدَّثَنَا مَعْمَرٌ، عَنْ هَمَّامِ بْنِ مُنَبِّهٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِمِثْلِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 919]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/919" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

## Narration 882

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A920)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا يَعْقُوبُ، - يَعْنِي ابْنَ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ - عَنْ سُهَيْلٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا قَالَ الْقَارِئُ غَيْرِ الْمَغْضُوبِ عَلَيْهِمْ وَلاَ الضَّالِّينَ ‏.‏ فَقَالَ مَنْ خَلْفَهُ آمِينَ ‏.‏ فَوَافَقَ قَوْلُهُ قَوْلَ أَهْلِ السَّمَاءِ غُفِرَ لَهُ مَا تَقَدَّمَ مِنْ ذَنْبِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 920]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/920" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 920</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   920 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: When the reciter (Imam) utters: &quot; Not of those on whom (is Thine) wrath and not the erring ones,&quot; and (the person) behind him utters Amin and his utterance synchronises with that of the dwellers of heavens, all his previous sins would be pardoned</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 920]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/920" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 920</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   920 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सुहैल के वलीद अबु सालेह ने हज़रात अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: जब करि (घेरिल मघदूबी अलैहिम वलद-डालीं) कहे और जो उसके पीछे है वह (भी) आमीन कहे और उसका कहना आसमान वालों की कही हुई (आमीन) के मुवाफ़िक़ हो जाये तो उसके पिछले गुनाह माफ कर दिए जाते हैं.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 920]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/920" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">سہیل کے والد ابو صالح نے حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی کہ رسول اللہﷺ نے فرمایا : ’’ جب قاری ﴿غير المغضوب عليهم والا الضالين﴾ کہے اور جو اس کے پیچھے ہے وہ ( بھی ) آمین کہے اور اس کا کہنا آسمان والوں کی کہی ہوئی ( آمین ) کے موافق ہو جائے تو اس کے پچھلے گناہ معاف کر دیے جاتے ہیں ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Suhail ke walid Abu Saleh ne Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Jab qari (Ghairil Maghdoobi Alaihim Walad-Dalleen) kahe aur jo uske peeche hai woh (bhi) Aameen kahe aur uska kehna aasman walon ki kahi hui (Aameen) ke muwafiq ho jaye to uske pichle gunah maaf kar diye jate hain.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 920]</p>

## Narration 883

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A921)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 921]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/921" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 921</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   921 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ يَحْيَى، وَقُتَيْبَةُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، وَأَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ وَعَمْرٌو النَّاقِدُ وَزُهَيْرُ بْنُ حَرْبٍ وَأَبُو كُرَيْبٍ جَمِيعًا عَنْ سُفْيَانَ، - قَالَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ بْنُ عُيَيْنَةَ، - عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، قَالَ سَمِعْتُ أَنَسَ بْنَ مَالِكٍ، يَقُولُ سَقَطَ النَّبِيُّ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنْ فَرَسٍ فَجُحِشَ شِقُّهُ الأَيْمَنُ فَدَخَلْنَا عَلَيْهِ نَعُودُهُ فَحَضَرَتِ الصَّلاَةُ فَصَلَّى بِنَا قَاعِدًا فَصَلَّيْنَا وَرَاءَهُ قُعُودًا فَلَمَّا قَضَى الصَّلاَةَ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّمَا جُعِلَ الإِمَامُ لِيُؤْتَمَّ بِهِ فَإِذَا كَبَّرَ فَكَبِّرُوا وَإِذَا سَجَدَ فَاسْجُدُوا وَإِذَا رَفَعَ فَارْفَعُوا وَإِذَا قَالَ سَمِعَ اللَّهُ لِمَنْ حَمِدَهُ ‏.‏ فَقُولُوا رَبَّنَا وَلَكَ الْحَمْدُ ‏.‏ وَإِذَا صَلَّى قَاعِدًا فَصَلُّوا قُعُودًا أَجْمَعُونَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/921" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Apostle of Allah in Anas account of the injury, prayer and instructions. The full field supplies the fall, visit, sitting prayer and instructions for following the imam, with the actual repeated formulas.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Anas bin Malik reported: The Apostle of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) fell down from a horse and his right side was grazed. We went to him to inquire after his health when the time of prayer came. He led us in prayer in a sitting posture and we said prayer behind him sitting, and when he finished the prayer hesaid: The Imam is appointed only to be followed; so when he recites takbir, you should also recite that; when he prostrates, you should also prostrate; when he rises up, you should also rise up, and when he said&quot; God listens to him who praises Him,&quot; you should say: &quot; Our Lord, to Thee be the praise,&quot; and when he prays sitting, all of you should pray sitting</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 921]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/921" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 921]</p>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 921</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   921 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">सुफ़यान बिन उययनह ने ज़ुहरी से हदीस बयान की, उन्होने कहा के मैने हज़रत अनस बिन मालिक (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से सुना, वो कहते थे के नबी (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) घोड़े से गिर गए तो आपका दायां पहलू छिल गया, हम आपके यहां आपकी अयादत के लिए हाज़िर हुए, नमाज़ का वक़्त हो गया तो आपने हमें बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ाई, चुनांचे हमने आपके पीछे बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ी, फिर जब आपने नमाज़ पूरी की तो फ़रमाया: इमाम इसी लिए बनाया गया के उसकी इक़्तिदा की जाए, चुनांचे जब वो तकबीर कहे तो तुम तकबीर कहो, जब वो सजदा करे तो तुम सजदा करो, जब वो (सर) उठाए तो तुम (सर) उठाओ और जब वो समी अल्लाहहु लिमन हमिदह (अल्लाह ने उसे सुन लिया जिसने उसकी हमद बयान की) कहे तो तुम रब्बना लकल हमद (ऐ हमारे रब! तेरी ही लिए सब तारीफ़ है) कहो और जब वो बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़े तो तुम भी बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ो।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/921" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 921]</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   921 ]</p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">سفیان بن عیینہ نے زہری سے حدیث بیان کی ، انہوں نے کہا کہ میں نے حضرت انس بن مالک ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے سنا ، وہ کہتے تھے کہ نبیﷺ گھوڑے سے گر گئے تو آپ کا دایاں پہلو چھل گیا ، ہم آپ کے ہاں آپ کی عیادت کے لیے حاضر ہوئے ، نماز کا وقت ہو گیا تو آپ نے ہمیں بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھائی ، چنانچھ ہم نے ( آپ کے اشارے پر ، حدیث 926 ۔ 928 ) آپ کے پیچھے بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھی ، پھر جب آپ نے نماز پوری کی تو فرمایا : ’’امام اسی لیے بنایا گیا کہ اس کی اقتدا کی جائے ، چنانچہ جب وہ تکبیر کہے تو تم تکبیر کہو ، جب وہ سجدہ کرے تو تم سجدہ کرو ، جب وہ ( سر ) اٹھائے تو تم ( سر ) اٹھاؤ اور جب وہ سمع الله لمن حمده ( اللہ نے اسے سن لیا جس نے اس کی حمد بیان کی ) کہے تو تم ربنا لك الحمد ( اے ہمارے رب! تیری ہی لیے سب تعریف ہے ) کہو اور جب وہ بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھے تو تم بھی بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھو ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/921" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/921" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Sufyan bin Uyaynah ne Zuhri se hadees bayan ki, unhone kaha ke maine Hazrat Anas bin Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) se suna, woh kehte the ke Nabi (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ghode se gir gaye to aapka dayaan pehlu chhil gaya, hum aapke yahan aapki ayadat ke liye hazir huye, namaz ka waqt ho gaya to aapne humein baith kar namaz padhayi, chunanche humne aapke peeche baith kar namaz padhi, phir jab aapne namaz puri ki to farmaya: Imam isi liye banaya gaya ke uski iqtida ki jaye, chunanche jab woh takbeer kahe to tum takbeer kaho, jab woh sajda kare to tum sajda karo, jab woh (sar) uthaye to tum (sar) uthao aur jab woh Samee Allahu Liman Hamidah (Allah ne use sun liya jisne uski hamd bayan ki) kahe to tum Rabbana Lakal Hamd (Ae hamare Rab! Teri hi liye sab tareef hai) kaho aur jab woh baith kar namaz padhe to tum bhi baith kar namaz padho.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/921" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

## Narration 884

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A922)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 922]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/922" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ رُمْحٍ، أَخْبَرَنَا اللَّيْثُ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ أَنَسِ بْنِ مَالِكٍ، قَالَ خَرَّ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم عَنْ فَرَسٍ فَجُحِشَ فَصَلَّى لَنَا قَاعِدًا ‏.‏ ثُمَّ ذَكَرَ نَحْوَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/922" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>

## Narration 885

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A923)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي حَرْمَلَةُ بْنُ يَحْيَى، أَخْبَرَنَا ابْنُ وَهْبٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي يُونُسُ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، أَخْبَرَنِي أَنَسُ بْنُ مَالِكٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم صُرِعَ عَنْ فَرَسٍ فَجُحِشَ شِقُّهُ الأَيْمَنُ بِنَحْوِ حَدِيثِهِمَا وَزَادَ ‏ &quot;‏ فَإِذَا صَلَّى قَائِمًا فَصَلُّوا قِيَامًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 923]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/923" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 923</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   923 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Anas bin Malik reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) fell down from a horse and his right side was grazed, and the rest of the hadith is the same with the addition of these words: &quot; When he (the Imam) says prayer standing, you should also do so</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 923]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/923" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 923</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   923 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">यूनुस ने इब्न-ए-शिहाब (ज़ुहरी) से रिवायत की, उन्होने कहा: मुझे हज़रत अनस (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) ने ख़बर दी के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) घोड़े से गिर गए और आपका दायां पहलू छिल गया... फिर उन दोनों हज़रात (सुफ़यान और लैथ) की रिवायत के मानिंद रिवायत की, अलबत्ता यूनुस ने ये इज़ाफ़ा किया: और जब वो (इमाम) खड़ा हो कर नमाज़ पढ़े तो तुम भी खड़े हो कर नमाज़ पढ़ो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 923]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/923" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">یونس نے ابن شہاب ( زہری ) سے روایت کی ، انہوں نے کہا : مجھے حضرت انس ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ نے خبر دی کہ رسول اللہﷺ گھوڑے سے گر گئے اور آپ کا دایاں پہلو چھل گیا ... پھر ان دونوں حضرات ( سفیان اور لیث ) کی روایت کے مانند روایت کی ، البتہ یونس نے یہ اضافہ کیا : ’’ اور جب وہ ( امام ) کھڑا ہو کر نماز پڑھے تو تم بھی کھڑے ہو کر نماز پڑھو</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 923]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/923" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Yunus ne Ibn-e-Shihab (Zuhri) se riwayat ki, unhone kaha: Mujhe Hazrat Anas (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne khabar di ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ghode se gir gaye aur aapka dayaan pehlu chhil gaya... phir un dono hazraat (Sufyan aur Laith) ki riwayat ke manind riwayat ki, albatta Yunus ne ye izafa kiya: Aur jab woh (Imam) khada ho kar namaz padhe to tum bhi khade ho kar namaz padho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 923]</p>

## Narration 886

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A924)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ أَبِي عُمَرَ، حَدَّثَنَا مَعْنُ بْنُ عِيسَى، عَنْ مَالِكِ بْنِ أَنَسٍ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، عَنْ أَنَسٍ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم رَكِبَ فَرَسًا فَصُرِعَ عَنْهُ فَجُحِشَ شِقُّهُ الأَيْمَنُ ‏.‏ بِنَحْوِ حَدِيثِهِمْ وَفِيهِ ‏ &quot;‏ إِذَا صَلَّى قَائِمًا فَصَلُّوا قِيَامًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 924]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/924" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 924</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   924 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Anas reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) rode a horse and fell down from it and his right side was grazed, and the rest of the hadith is the same, and (these words) are found in it: &quot; When he (the Imam) says prayer in an erect posture, you should also say it in an erect posture</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 924]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/924" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मालिक बिन अनस ने ज़ुहरी से और उन्होंने हज़रात अनस बिन मालिक (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) घोड़े पर सवार हुए और उस से गिर पड़े तीनो रावियों की तरह रिवायत की और इस में (भी ये) है: जब वह (इमाम) खड़ा हो कर नमाज़ पढ़े तो तुम भी खड़े हो कर नमाज़ पढ़ो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 924]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/924" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">مالک بن انس نے زہری سے اور انہوں نے حضرت انس بن مالک ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی کہ رسول اللہﷺ گھوڑے پر سوار ہوئے اور اس سے گر پڑے ، اس سے آپ کا دایاں پہلو چھل گیا .... آگے مذکورہ بالا تینوں راویوں کی طرح روایت کی اور اس میں ( بھی یہ ) ہے : ’’ جب وہ ( امام ) کھڑا ہو کر نماز پڑھے تو تم بھی کھڑے ہو کر نماز پڑھو ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 924]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/924" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="ltr">Malik bin Anas ne Zuhri se aur unhone Hazrat Anas bin Malik (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ghode par sawar huye aur us se gir pade, is se aapka dayaan pehlu chhil gaya... aage mazkoora bala teeno raviyon ki tarah riwayat ki aur is mein (bhi ye) hai: Jab woh (Imam) khada ho kar namaz padhe to tum bhi khade ho kar namaz padho.</p>

## Narration 887

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A925)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ بْنُ حُمَيْدٍ، أَخْبَرَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّزَّاقِ، أَخْبَرَنَا مَعْمَرٌ، عَنِ الزُّهْرِيِّ، أَخْبَرَنِي أَنَسٌ، أَنَّ النَّبِيَّ صلى الله عليه وسلم سَقَطَ مِنْ فَرَسِهِ فَجُحِشَ شِقُّهُ الأَيْمَنُ ‏.‏ وَسَاقَ الْحَدِيثَ وَلَيْسَ فِيهِ زِيَادَةُ يُونُسَ وَمَالِكٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 925]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/925" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Anas bin Malik reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) fell down from his horse and his right side was grazed, and the rest of the hadith is the same. In this hadith there are no additions (of words) as transmitted by Yunus and Malik</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 925]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/925" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 888

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A926)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدَةُ بْنُ سُلَيْمَانَ، عَنْ هِشَامٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ عَائِشَةَ، قَالَتِ اشْتَكَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَدَخَلَ عَلَيْهِ نَاسٌ مِنْ أَصْحَابِهِ يَعُودُونَهُ فَصَلَّى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم جَالِسًا فَصَلَّوْا بِصَلاَتِهِ قِيَامًا فَأَشَارَ إِلَيْهِمْ أَنِ اجْلِسُوا ‏.‏ فَجَلَسُوا فَلَمَّا انْصَرَفَ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّمَا جُعِلَ الإِمَامُ لِيُؤْتَمَّ بِهِ فَإِذَا رَكَعَ فَارْكَعُوا وَإِذَا رَفَعَ فَارْفَعُوا وَإِذَا صَلَّى جَالِسًا فَصَلُّوا جُلُوسًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 926]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/926" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 926</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   926 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">A&#x27;isha reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) fell ill and some of his Companions came to inquire after his health. The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said prayer sitting, while (his Companions) said it (behind him) standing. He (the Holy Prophet) directed them by his gesture to sit down, and they sat down (in prayer). After finishing the (prayer) he (the Holy Prophet) said: The Imam is appointed so that he should be followed, so bow down when he bows down, and rise upp when he rises up and say (prayer) sitting when he (the Imam) says (it) sitting</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 926]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/926" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

</details>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दह बिन सुलैमान ने हिशाम (बिन उरवह) से रिवायत की, उन्होने अपने वालिद से और उन्होने हज़रत आइशा (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हा) से रिवायत की के रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) बीमार हो गए, आपके सहाबा में से कुछ लोग आपके पास आपकी बीमार पुर्सी के लिए हाज़िर हुए। रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ी तो उन्होने आपकी इक़्तिदा में खड़े हो कर नमाज़ पढ़नी शुरू की। आपने उन्हें इशारा किया के बैठ जाओ तो वो बैठ गए। जब आप नमाज़ से फ़ारिग़ हो गए तो आपने फ़रमाया: इमाम इसी लिए बनाया जाता है के उसकी इक़्तिदा की जाए, जब वो रुकू करे तो तुम रुकू करो और जब वो (रुकू-ओ-सुजूद से सर) उठाए तो (फिर) तुम (भी सर) उठाओ और जब वो बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़े तो तुम भी बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 926]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/926" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبدہ بن سلیمان نے ہشام ( بن عروہ ) سے روایت کی ، انہوں نے اپنے والد سے اور انہوں نے حضرت عائشہ رضی اللہ عنہا سے روایت کی کہ رسول اللہﷺ بیمار ہو گئے ، آپ کے صحابہ میں سے کچھ لوگ آپ کے پاس آپ کی بیمار پرسی کے لیے حاضر ہوئے ۔ رسول اللہﷺ نے بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھی تو انہوں نے آپ کی اقتدا میں کھڑے ہو کر نماز پڑھنی شروع کی ۔ آپ نے انہیں اشارہ کیا کہ بیٹھ جاؤ تو وہ بیٹھ گئے ۔ جب آپ نماز سے فارغ ہو گئے تو آپ نے فرمایا : ’’ امام اسی لیے بنایا جاتا ہے کہ اس کی اقتدا کی جائے ، جب وہ رکوع کرے تو تم رکوع کرو اور جب وہ ( رکوع وسجود سے سر ) اٹھائے تو ( پھر ) تم ( بھی سر ) اٹھاؤ اور جب وہ بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھے تو تم بھی بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھو ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 926]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/926" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdah bin Sulaiman ne Hisham (bin Urwah) se riwayat ki, unhone apne walid se aur unhone Hazrat Aisha (Radi Allahu Anha) se riwayat ki ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) bimar ho gaye, aapke sahaba mein se kuch log aapke paas aapki bimar pursi ke liye hazir huye. Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne baith kar namaz padhi to unhone aapki iqtida mein khade ho kar namaz padhni shuru ki. Aapne unhein ishara kiya ke baith jao to woh baith gaye. Jab aap namaz se farigh ho gaye to aapne farmaya: Imam isi liye banaya jata hai ke uski iqtida ki jaye, jab woh ruku kare to tum ruku karo aur jab woh (ruku-o-sujood se sar) uthaye to (phir) tum (bhi sar) uthao aur jab woh baith kar namaz padhe to tum bhi baith kar namaz padho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 926]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/926" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو الرَّبِيعِ الزَّهْرَانِيُّ، حَدَّثَنَا حَمَّادٌ يَعْنِي ابْنَ زَيْدٍ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، وَأَبُو كُرَيْبٍ قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ نُمَيْرٍ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ نُمَيْرٍ، قَالَ حَدَّثَنَا أَبِي جَمِيعًا، عَنْ هِشَامِ بْنِ عُرْوَةَ، بِهَذَا الإِسْنَادِ نَحْوَهُ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا لَيْثٌ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ رُمْحٍ، أَخْبَرَنَا اللَّيْثُ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ جَابِرٍ، قَالَ اشْتَكَى رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم فَصَلَّيْنَا وَرَاءَهُ وَهُوَ قَاعِدٌ وَأَبُو بَكْرٍ يُسْمِعُ النَّاسَ تَكْبِيرَهُ فَالْتَفَتَ إِلَيْنَا فَرَآنَا قِيَامًا فَأَشَارَ إِلَيْنَا فَقَعَدْنَا فَصَلَّيْنَا بِصَلاَتِهِ قُعُودًا فَلَمَّا سَلَّمَ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنْ كِدْتُمْ آنِفًِا لَتَفْعَلُونَ فِعْلَ فَارِسَ وَالرُّومِ يَقُومُونَ عَلَى مُلُوكِهِمْ وَهُمْ قُعُودٌ فَلاَ تَفْعَلُوا ائْتَمُّوا بِأَئِمَّتِكُمْ إِنْ صَلَّى قَائِمًا فَصَلُّوا قِيَامًا وَإِنْ صَلَّى قَاعِدًا فَصَلُّوا قُعُودًا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/928" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah in Jabirs account of the sitting prayer and explanation. The complete field supplies the illness, Abu Bakrs audible takbir, the gesture to sit and the concluding instructions and comparison. Preserve this historical source statement without a new bank directive.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) was ill and we said prayer behind him and he was sitting. And Abu Bakr was making audible to the people his takbir. As he paid his attention towards us he saw us standing and (directed us to sit down) with a gesture. So we sat down and said our prayer with his prayer in a sitting posture. After uttering salutation he said: You were at this time about to do an act like that of the Persians and the Romans. They stand before their kings while they sit, so don&#x27;t do that; follow your Imams. If they say prayer standing, you should also do so, and if they say prayer sitting, you should also say prayer sitting</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 928]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/928" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">लैथ ने अबू जुबैर से और उन्होंने हज़रात जाबिर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) की बीमारी में हमने आपके पीछे इस तरह नमाज़ पढ़ी के आपने बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ाई और हज़रात सिद्दीक़-इ-अकबर (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) मुकब्बिर की हैसियत में तकबीरत कहते थे. नमाज़ में हमें खड़ा देख कर आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने हमें बैठने का हुकुम दिया तो हम बैठ गए हुआ तुमने इस वक़्त वह काम किया जैसा के फारस और रोम वाले अपने बादशाह के सामने खड़े रहते हैं और बादशाह बैठा रहता है. अब आइंदा ऐसा न करना बालकः हमेशा अपने इमाम की पैरवी करो अगर वह बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ाये तो तुम भी बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ो.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/928" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">۔ لیث نے ابو زبیر سے اور انہوں نے حضرت جابر ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی کہ رسول اللہﷺ كی ‌بیماری ‌میں ‌ہم ‌نے ‌آپ ‌كے ‌پیچھے ‌اس ‌طرح ‌نماز ‌پڑھی ‌كہ ‌آپ ‌نے ‌بیٹھ ‌كر ‌نماز ‌پڑھائی ‌اور ‌حضرت ‌صدیق ‌اكبر ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌مكبر ‌كی ‌حیثیت ‌میں ‌تكبیرات ‌كہتے ‌تھے ‌. ‌نماز ‌میں ‌ہمیں ‌كھڑا ‌دیكھ ‌كر ‌آپ ‌صلی ‌اللہ ‌علیہ ‌وسلم ‌نے ‌ہمیں ‌بیٹھنے ‌كا ‌حكم ‌دیا ‌تو ‌ہم ‌بیٹھ ‌گئے ‌پھر ‌بعد ‌فراغت ‌نماز ‌ارشاد ‌عالی ‌ہوا ‌تم ‌نے ‌اس ‌وقت ‌وہ ‌كام ‌كیا ‌جیسا ‌كہ ‌فارس ‌اور ‌روم ‌والے ‌اپنے ‌بادشاہ ‌كے ‌سامنے ‌كھڑے ‌رہتے ‌ہیں ‌اور ‌بادشاہ ‌بیٹھا ‌رہتا ‌ہے ‌. ‌اب ‌آئندہ ‌ایسا ‌نہ ‌كرنا ‌بلكہ ‌ہمیشہ ‌اپنے ‌امام ‌كی ‌پیروی ‌كرو ‌اگر ‌وہ ‌بیٹھ ‌كر ‌نماز ‌پڑھائے ‌تو ‌تم ‌بھی ‌بیٹھ ‌كر ‌نماز ‌پڑھو ‌</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Laith ne Abu Zubair se aur unhone Hazrat Jabir (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki ke Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ki bimari mein humne aapke peeche is tarah namaz padhi ke aapne baith kar namaz padhayi aur Hazrat Siddiq-e-Akbar (Radi Allahu Anhu) mukabbir ki haisiyat mein takbeerat kehte the. Namaz mein humein khada dekh kar aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne humein baithne ka hukum diya to hum baith gaye, phir baad faraghat-e-namaz irshad-e-aali hua tumne is waqt woh kaam kiya jaisa ke Fars aur Room wale apne badshah ke samne khade rehte hain aur badshah baitha rehta hai. Ab aayinda aisa na karna balkeh hamesha apne imam ki pairvi karo agar woh baith kar namaz padhaye to tum bhi baith kar namaz padho.</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/928" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا يَحْيَى بْنُ يَحْيَى، أَخْبَرَنَا حُمَيْدُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ الرُّؤَاسِيُّ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي الزُّبَيْرِ، عَنْ جَابِرٍ، قَالَ صَلَّى بِنَا رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم وَأَبُو بَكْرٍ خَلْفَهُ فَإِذَا كَبَّرَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم كَبَّرَ أَبُو بَكْرٍ لِيُسْمِعَنَا ‏.‏ ثُمَّ ذَكَرَ نَحْوَ حَدِيثِ اللَّيْثِ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: prophetic report. Attribution: The Messenger of Allah in Jabirs account, with Abu Bakr relaying the takbir. Select the actual prayer and audible-takbir episode. The pointer to Laiths remaining version stays context.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Jabir said: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) led the prayer and Abu Bakr was behind him. When the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) recited the takbir, Abu Bakr also recited (it) in order to make it audible to us. </p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 929]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/929" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 892

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A930)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ بْنُ سَعِيدٍ، حَدَّثَنَا الْمُغِيرَةُ، - يَعْنِي الْحِزَامِيَّ - عَنْ أَبِي الزِّنَادِ، عَنِ الأَعْرَجِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، أَنَّ رَسُولَ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّمَا الإِمَامُ لِيُؤْتَمَّ بِهِ فَلاَ تَخْتَلِفُوا عَلَيْهِ فَإِذَا كَبَّرَ فَكَبِّرُوا وَإِذَا رَكَعَ فَارْكَعُوا وَإِذَا قَالَ سَمِعَ اللَّهُ لِمَنْ حَمِدَهُ ‏.‏ فَقُولُوا اللَّهُمَّ رَبَّنَا لَكَ الْحَمْدُ ‏.‏ وَإِذَا سَجَدَ فَاسْجُدُوا وَإِذَا صَلَّى جَالِسًا فَصَلُّوا جُلُوسًا أَجْمَعُونَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 930]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/930" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 930</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   930 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: The Imam is appointed, so that he should be followed, so don&#x27;t be at variance with him. Recite takbir when he recites it; bow down when he bows down and when he says: &quot; Allah listens to him who praises Him,&quot; say: &quot; O Allah, our Lord, to Thee be the Praise.&quot; And when he (the Imam) prostrates, you should also prostrate, and when he says prayer sitting, you should all observe prayer sitting</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 930]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/930" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   930 ]</p>

</details>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">आ&#x27;राज ने हज़रात अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: इमाम इक़्तेदा ही के लिए बनाया गया है कहो और जब वह रुकू करे तो तुम रुकू करो और जब वह समय-अल्लाह-ु-लिमन-हमीदह कहे तो तुम अल्लाहुम्मा रब्बना लकल हम्द कहो और जब वह सजदा करे तो तुम सज्दा करो और जब वह बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़े तो तुम भी बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ो.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 930]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/930" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">اعرج نے حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی کہ رسول اللہﷺ نے فرمایا : ’’امام اقتدا ہی کے لیے بنایا گیا ہے ، اس لیے اس کی مخالفت نہ کرو ، چنانچہ جب وہ تکبیر کہے تو تم تکبیر کہو اور جب وہ رکوع کرے تو تم رکوع کرو اور جب وہ سمع الله لمن حمده كہے تو تم اللهم ، ربنا لك الحمد کہو اور جب وہ سجدہ کرے تو تم سجدہ کرو اور جب وہ بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھے تو تم بھی بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھو ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 930]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/930" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">A&#x27;raj ne Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki ki Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Imam iqteda hi ke liye banaya gaya hai, isliye uski mukhalifat na karo, chunancha jab woh takbeer kahe to tum takbeer kaho aur jab woh ruku kare to tum ruku karo aur jab woh sami-Allah-u-liman-hamidah kahe to tum Allahumma Rabbana lakal hamd kaho aur jab woh sajda kare to tum sajda karo aur jab woh baith kar namaz padhe to tum bhi baith kar namaz padho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 930]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/930" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 893

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A931)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ رَافِعٍ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الرَّزَّاقِ، حَدَّثَنَا مَعْمَرٌ، عَنْ هَمَّامِ بْنِ مُنَبِّهٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِمِثْلِهِ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 931]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/931" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 931</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   931 ]</p>

</details>

<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">A hadith like this has been transmitted by Hammam bin Munabbih from the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) on the authority of Abu Huraira</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 931]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/931" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">हुमाम बिन मुनब्बह ने हज़रत अबू हुरैरह (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) से और उन्होंने नबी अकरम (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से इसी (साबिक़ा हदीस) के मानिंद रिवायत बयान की.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 931]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/931" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ہمام بن منبہ نے حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے اور انہوں نے نبی اکرمﷺ سے اسی ( سابقہ حدیث ) کے مانند روایت بیان کی ۔</p>

## Narration 894

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A932)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا إِسْحَاقُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ، وَابْنُ، خَشْرَمٍ قَالاَ أَخْبَرَنَا عِيسَى بْنُ يُونُسَ، حَدَّثَنَا الأَعْمَشُ، عَنْ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ كَانَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم يُعَلِّمُنَا يَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ لاَ تُبَادِرُوا الإِمَامَ إِذَا كَبَّرَ فَكَبِّرُوا وَإِذَا قَالَ وَلاَ الضَّالِّينَ ‏.‏ فَقُولُوا آمِينَ ‏.‏ وَإِذَا رَكَعَ فَارْكَعُوا وَإِذَا قَالَ سَمِعَ اللَّهُ لِمَنْ حَمِدَهُ ‏.‏ فَقُولُوا اللَّهُمَّ رَبَّنَا لَكَ الْحَمْدُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 932]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/932" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 932</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   932 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) while teaching us (the principles of faith), said: Do not try to go ahead of the Imam, recite takbir when he recites it, and when he says: &quot;Nor of those who err&quot; you should say Amin, bow down when he bows down, and when he says: &quot;Allah listens to him who praises Him&quot; say: &quot;O Allah, our Lord, to Thee be the praise</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 932]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/932" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 932</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   932 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अ&#x27;मश ने अबू सालेह से और उन्होने हज़रत अबू हुरैराह (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से रिवायत की, उन्होने कहा: रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) हमें ता&#x27;लीम देते थे, फ़रमाते थे: इमाम से आगे न बढ़ो, जब वो तकबीर कहे तो तुम तकबीर कहो, जब वो (वलद-दल्लीन) कहे तो तुम आमीन कहो और जब वो रुकू करे तो तुम रुकू करो और जब वो समी-अल्लाह-उ-लिमन-हमिदह कहे तो तुम अल्लाहुम्मा रब्बना लकल हमद कहो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 932]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/932" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">اعمش نے ابو صالح سے اور انہوں نے حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے روایت کی ، انہوں نے کہا : رسول اللہﷺ ہمیں تعلیم دیتے تھے ، فرماتے تھے : ’’ امام سے آگے نہ بڑھو ، جب وہ تکبیر کہے تو تم تکبیر کہو ، جب وہ ﴿ولا الضالين﴾ کہے تو تم آمین کہو اور جب وہ رکوع کرے تو تم رکوع کرو اور جب وہ سمع الله لمن حمده کہے تو تم اللهم ، ربنا لك الحمد کہو ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 932]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/932" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">A&#x27;mash ne Abu Saleh se aur unhone Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se riwayat ki, unhone kaha: Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) hamein ta&#x27;leem dete thay, farmate thay: Imam se aage na badho, jab woh takbeer kahe to tum takbeer kaho, jab woh (Walad-dalleen) kahe to tum Aameen kaho aur jab woh ruku kare to tum ruku karo aur jab woh sami-Allah-u-liman-hamidah kahe to tum Allahumma Rabbana lakal hamd kaho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 932]</p>

## Narration 895

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A933)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا قُتَيْبَةُ، حَدَّثَنَا عَبْدُ الْعَزِيزِ، - يَعْنِي الدَّرَاوَرْدِيَّ - عَنْ سُهَيْلِ بْنِ أَبِي صَالِحٍ، عَنْ أَبِيهِ، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صلى الله عليه وسلم بِنَحْوِهِ إِلاَّ قَوْلَهُ ‏&quot;‏ وَلاَ الضَّالِّينَ ‏.‏ فَقُولُوا آمِينَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏ وَزَادَ ‏&quot;‏ وَلاَ تَرْفَعُوا قَبْلَهُ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 933]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/933" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 933</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   933 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira reported from the Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) (a hadith) like it, except the words: &quot; Nor of those who err, say Amin&quot; and added: &quot; And don&#x27;t rise up ahead of him</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 933]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/933" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 933</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   933 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">सुहैल बिन अबी सालेह ने अपने वालिद (अबू सालेह) से, उन्होने हज़रत अबू हुरैराह (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से और उन्होने नबी (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से इसी (साबिक़ा हदीस) के हम-मअनी रिवायत की, सिवाये इस हिस्से के: जब वो (वलद-दल्लीन) कहे तो तुम आमीन कहो, और ये हिस्सा बढ़ाया: और तुम उस से पहले (सर) न उठाओ।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 933]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/933" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">۔ سہیل بن ابی صالح نے اپنے والد ( ابو صالح ) سے ، انہوں نے حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے اور انہوں نے نبیﷺ سے اسی ( سابقہ حدیث ) کے ہم معنیٰ روایت کی ، سوائے اس حصے کے : ’’ جب وہ ﴿ولا الضالين﴾ کہے تو تم آمین کہو ‘ ‘ اور یہ حصہ بڑھایا : ’’ اور تم اس سے پہلے ( سر ) نہ اٹھاؤ ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 933]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/933" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Suhail bin Abi Saleh ne apne walid (Abu Saleh) se, unhone Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se aur unhone Nabi (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se isi (sabiqa hadees) ke ham-ma&#x27;ni riwayat ki, siwaye is hisse ke: Jab woh (Walad-dalleen) kahe to tum Aameen kaho, aur yeh hissa badhaya: Aur tum us se pehle (sar) na uthao.</p>

## Narration 896

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A934)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ بَشَّارٍ، حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ جَعْفَرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، ح وَحَدَّثَنَا عُبَيْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ مُعَاذٍ، - وَاللَّفْظُ لَهُ - حَدَّثَنَا أَبِي، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ يَعْلَى، - وَهُوَ ابْنُ عَطَاءٍ - سَمِعَ أَبَا عَلْقَمَةَ، سَمِعَ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ، يَقُولُ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّمَا الإِمَامُ جُنَّةٌ فَإِذَا صَلَّى قَاعِدًا فَصَلُّوا قُعُودًا وَإِذَا قَالَ سَمِعَ اللَّهُ لِمَنْ حَمِدَهُ ‏.‏ فَقُولُوا اللَّهُمَّ رَبَّنَا لَكَ الْحَمْدُ ‏.‏ فَإِذَا وَافَقَ قَوْلُ أَهْلِ الأَرْضِ قَوْلَ أَهْلِ السَّمَاءِ غُفِرَ لَهُ مَا تَقَدَّمَ مِنْ ذَنْبِهِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 934]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/934" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira reported: The Messenger of Allah (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) said: Verily the Imam is a shield, say prayer sitting when he says prayer sitting. And when he says: &quot; Allah listens to him who praises Him,&quot; say: &quot; O Allah, our Lord, to Thee be the praise.&quot; and when the utterance of the people of the earth synchronises with that of the beings of heaven (angels), all the previous sins would be pardoned</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 934]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/934" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू अलक़मा ने हज़रत अबू हुरैराह (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) से सुना, कह रहे थे: रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: यक़ीनन इमाम एक ढाल है (तुम उसके पीछे पीछे रहो), चुनांचा जब वो बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़े तो तुम भी बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ो और जब वो समी-अल्लाह-उ-लिमन-हमिदह कहे तो तुम अल्लाहुम्मा रब्बना लकल हमद कहो क्यूंकि जब ज़मीन वाले का कहा हुआ आसमान वालों के कहने के मुवाफ़िक़ हो जायेगा तो उसके साबिक़ा गुनाह बख्श दिए जाएंगे।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 934]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/934" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابو علقمہ نے حضرت ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ سے سنا ، کہہ رہے تھے : رسول اللہﷺ نے فرمایا : ’’ یقینا امام ایک ڈھال ہے ( تم اس کے پیچھے پیچھے رہو ) ، چنانچہ جب وہ بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھے تو تم بھی بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھو اور جب وہ سمع الله لمن حمده كہے تو تم اللهم ، ربنا لك الحمد کہو کیونکہ جب زمین والے کا کہا ہوا آسمان والوں کے کہنے کے موافق ہو جائے گا تو اس کے سابقہ گناہ بخش دیے جائیں گے ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 934]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/934" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Alqama ne Hazrat Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) se suna, keh rahe thay: Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Yaqeenan imam ek dhaal hai (tum uske peeche peeche raho), chunancha jab woh baith kar namaz padhe to tum bhi baith kar namaz padho aur jab woh sami-Allah-u-liman-hamidah kahe to tum Allahumma Rabbana lakal hamd kaho kyunki jab zameen wale ka kaha hua aasman walon ke kehne ke muwafiq ho jayega to uske sabiqa gunah baksh diye jayenge.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 934]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/934" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 897

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-muslim%3A935)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 4</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنِي أَبُو الطَّاهِرِ، حَدَّثَنَا ابْنُ وَهْبٍ، عَنْ حَيْوَةَ، أَنَّ أَبَا يُونُسَ، مَوْلَى أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ حَدَّثَهُ قَالَ سَمِعْتُ أَبَا هُرَيْرَةَ، يَقُولُ عَنْ رَسُولِ اللَّهِ صلى الله عليه وسلم أَنَّهُ قَالَ ‏ &quot;‏ إِنَّمَا جُعِلَ الإِمَامُ لِيُؤْتَمَّ بِهِ فَإِذَا كَبَّرَ فَكَبِّرُوا وَإِذَا رَكَعَ فَارْكَعُوا وَإِذَا قَالَ سَمِعَ اللَّهُ لِمَنْ حَمِدَهُ ‏.‏ فَقُولُوا اللَّهُمَّ رَبَّنَا لَكَ الْحَمْدُ ‏.‏ وَإِذَا صَلَّى قَائِمًا فَصَلُّوا قِيَامًا وَإِذَا صَلَّى قَاعِدًا فَصَلُّوا قُعُودًا أَجْمَعُونَ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 935]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/935" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 935</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   935 ]</p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Huraira reported Allah&#x27;s Messenger (peace and blessings of Allah be upon him) saying: The Imam is appointed to be followed. So recite takbir when he recites it, and bow down when he bows down and when he utters: &quot; Allah listens to him who praises Him,&quot; say&quot; O Allah, our Lord, for Thee be the praise.&quot; And when he prays, standing, you should pray standing. And when he prays sitting, all of you should pray sitting</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 935]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/935" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sahih Muslim · 935</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sahih Muslim   935 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैराह (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) के आज़ाद कर्दा ग़ुलाम अबू यूनुस ने हदीस बयान की, कहा: मैने अबू हुरैराह (रज़ि अल्लाहु अन्हु) को रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) से रिवायत करते सुना की आप (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: इमाम इसलिये बनाया गया है की उसकी पैरवी की जाए, जब वो तकबीर कहे तो तुम तकबीर कहो और जब वो रुकू करे तो तुम रुकू और जब वो समी-अल्लाह-उ-लिमन-हमिदह कहे तो तुम अल्लाहुम्मा रब्बना लकल हमद कहो, और जब वो खड़ा हो कर नमाज़ पढ़े तो तुम भी खड़े हो कर पढ़ो और जब वो बैठ कर पढ़े तो तुम भी सब के सब बैठ कर नमाज़ पढ़ो।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sahih Muslim · 935]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/935" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">(ابو علقمہ کے بجائے ) ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ کے آزاد کردہ غلام ابو یونس نے حدیث بیان کی ، کہا : میں نے ابو ہریرہ ‌رضی ‌اللہ ‌عنہ ‌ ‌ کو رسول اللہﷺ سے روایت کرتے سنا کہ آپﷺ نے فرمایا : ’’امام اس لیے بنایا گیا ہے کہ اس کی پیروی کی جائے ، جب وہ تکبیر کہے تو تم تکبیر کہو اور جب وہ رکوع کرے تو تم رکوع اور جب وہ سمع الله لمن حمده کہے تو تم اللهم ، ربنا لك الحمد کہو ، اور جب وہ کھڑا ہو کر نماز پڑھے تو تم بھی کھڑے ہو کر پڑھو اور جب وہ بیٹھ کر پڑھے تو تم بھی سب کے سب بیٹھ کر نماز پڑھو ۔ ‘ ‘</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sahih Muslim · 935]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/935" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke azaad karda ghulam Abu Yunus ne hadees bayan ki, kaha: Maine Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) ko Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) se riwayat karte suna ki aap (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ne farmaya: Imam isliye banaya gaya hai ki uski pairwi ki jaye, jab woh takbeer kahe to tum takbeer kaho aur jab woh ruku kare to tum ruku aur jab woh sami-Allah-u-liman-hamidah kahe to tum Allahumma Rabbana lakal hamd kaho, aur jab woh khada ho kar namaz padhe to tum bhi khade ho kar padho aur jab woh baith kar padhe to tum bhi sab ke sab baith kar namaz padho.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sahih Muslim · 935]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/muslim/935" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sahih Muslim</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Roman Urdu · references (1)</summary>
